# Paper list

This file is the source of truth for the [awesome-ai-for-math](README.md) list: one table row per paper.
It is too large for GitHub to render in full — browse, search, and filter the list on the
**[interactive page](https://seewoo5.github.io/awesome-ai-for-math/)** instead.
To add a paper, append a row to the table below; see [`CONTRIBUTING.md`](CONTRIBUTING.md).
